- **project-initializer**: Node.js/Next.js 스타터킷을 Chain of Thought 방식으로 분석해 프로덕션 준비 상태로 초기화·최적화하는 서브에이전트. 비대한 보일러플레이트를 제거하고 미사용 의존성을 정리한다. "프로젝트 초기화해줘", "스타터 템플릿 정리해줘", "프로덕션 준비 상태로 만들어줘" 같은 요청 시 사용. ([kep1ian-z/invoice-web](https://github.com/kep1ian-z/invoice-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-initializer.md))
- **project-initializer-zephyr**: You are a sub-agent of the `ti-ccstudio-migrate-to-ti` skill. Your job is to scaffold a fresh `west`/Zephyr application directory, create the markdown migration report, and graft the source project's files into it. You run in a fresh context. You take **no content inputs** beyond which plan step to read and the workspace path — you read everything from `{workspace}/migration-plan.md`. ([MidwestMotorBoy/Battery-Bank](https://github.com/MidwestMotorBoy/Battery-Bank/blob/HEAD/firmware/.claude/skills/ti-ccstudio-migrate-to-ti/agents/project-initializer-zephyr.md) · ★2)
- **project-initializer_cn**: [通用] 通用项目初始化编排器，支持 PLAN 和 APPLY 模式。PLAN：通过调用技能 01、06、07 生成项目初始化计划（Wiki + 构建）。APPLY：执行初始化（创建 Wiki 文件、构建脚本、CI/CD 配置）。使用场景：为任何项目类型初始化项目文档和构建自动化。需要技能 01~08 已存在（先运行 00-bootstrap-core-skills）。 ([miyamaerei/AutoCodeForge](https://github.com/miyamaerei/AutoCodeForge/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/project-initializer_cn.agent.md))
- **project-intake**: Interviews you when a project starts (or when the stack changes) and personalizes the swarm to it. Use right after vendoring AgentSmith into a repo, or any time you want to re-tighten the agents/skills. Writes .agentsmith/profile and prunes/tunes the agents to match your stack. ([HaimW/discogs-tool](https://github.com/HaimW/discogs-tool/blob/HEAD/.agentsmith/agents/project-intake.md) · 2 copies)

Writes plan/{slug}/integration.md and reports INTEGRATED, FAILED or BLOCKED. Never commits, never edits implementation code, never touches a live endpoint. Use from /glab:project-integrate, after the develop or analyze stage and before /glab:project-review. It runs on a read-only snapshot of the worktree, on a different agent or a more capable model than the designer, at most three rounds per level. Run by the Sebenza daemon through `sebenza-agentctl review`. ([groundtruthsystems/sebenza](https://github.com/groundtruthsystems/sebenza/blob/HEAD/skills/agents/project-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **project-reviewer**: Project level — Reviews changes against AGENTS.md and for correctness. Reads AGENTS.md first, then reports findings as file:line with the rule broken and the fix. Read-only — never edits, stages or commits. Use after writing or changing code, and before committing. ([glab-services/skills](https://github.com/glab-services/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/glab/agents/project-reviewer.md))
- **project-reviewer**: Use when reviewing a project, feature, release, or template cycle for delivery learnings, git-history patterns, improvement proposals, and first-draft-PR handoff.
